@code {

    private readonly List<CourseGridDto> _items = new();

    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _processing;

    private bool _showForm;
    private bool _editing;

    private string? _message;
    private string? _formError;

    private bool _isError;

    private int _page = 1;
    private const int PageSize = 20;

    private int _totalItems;
    private int _totalPages;


    private CreateCourseCommand _createModel = new();

    private UpdateCourseCommand _updateModel = new();


    /*
     * این propertyها باعث می‌شوند
     * فرم Create و Edit از یک markup استفاده کند.
     */

    private string _title
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.Title
            : _createModel.Title;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.Title = value;
            else
                _createModel.Title = value;
        }
    }

    private string _slug
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.Slug
            : _createModel.Slug;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.Slug = value;
            else
                _createModel.Slug = value;
        }
    }

    private string? _description
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.Description
            : _createModel.Description;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.Description = value;
            else
                _createModel.Description = value;
        }
    }

    private string? _coverImageUrl
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.CoverImageUrl
            : _createModel.CoverImageUrl;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.CoverImageUrl = value;
            else
                _createModel.CoverImageUrl = value;
        }
    }

    private bool _isPublished
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.IsPublished
            : _createModel.IsPublished;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.IsPublished = value;
            else
                _createModel.IsPublished = value;
        }
    }

    private bool _isInEnglish
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.IsInEnglish
            : _createModel.IsInEnglish;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.IsInEnglish = value;
            else
                _createModel.IsInEnglish = value;
        }
    }

    private int _order
    {
        get => _editing
            ? _updateModel.OrderInCourses
            : _createModel.OrderInCourses;

        set
        {
            if (_editing)
                _updateModel.OrderInCourses = value;
            else
                _createModel.OrderInCourses = value;
        }
    }


    private object _form =>
        _editing
            ? _updateModel
            : _createModel;


    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        await LoadPage(1);
    }


    private async Task LoadPage(int page)
    {
        _loading = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            var result = await GridHandler.Invoke(
                new GetCourseListAsGridQuery
                {
                    Page = page,
                    PageSize = PageSize
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            _items.Clear();

            if (result.Data is not null)
                _items.AddRange(result.Data);

            _page = page;

            _totalItems = result.Total;

            _totalPages = (int)Math.Ceiling(
                (double)_totalItems / PageSize);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }


    private void ShowCreate()
    {
        _editing = false;

        _createModel = new CreateCourseCommand
        {
            OrderInCourses = 0,
            IsPublished = false,
            IsInEnglish = false
        };

        _formError = null;
        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }


    private void ShowEdit(CourseGridDto item)
    {
        _editing = true;

        _updateModel = new UpdateCourseCommand
        {
            Id = item.Id,
            Title = item.Title,
            Slug = item.Slug,
            Description = item.Description,
            CoverImageUrl = item.CoverImageUrl,
            IsPublished = item.IsPublished,
            IsInEnglish = item.IsInEnglish,
            OrderInCourses = item.OrderInCourses
        };

        _formError = null;
        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }


    private void CloseForm()
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _showForm = false;
        _formError = null;
    }


    private async Task Save()
    {
        _processing = true;
        _formError = null;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            if (_editing)
            {
                await UpdateHandler.Invoke(
                    _updateModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);

                ShowSuccess(
                    "دوره با موفقیت ویرایش شد.");
            }
            else
            {
                await CreateHandler.Invoke(
                    _createModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);

                ShowSuccess(
                    "دوره با موفقیت ایجاد شد.");
            }

            _showForm = false;

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _formError = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }


    private async Task Delete(CourseGridDto item)
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _processing = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            await DeleteHandler.Invoke(
                new DeleteCourseCommand
                {
                    Id = item.Id
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            ShowSuccess(
                "دوره با موفقیت حذف شد.");

            if (_items.Count == 1 && _page > 1)
                _page--;

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }


    private void ShowSuccess(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = false;
    }


    private void ShowError(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = true;
    }

}

@code {
    private CreatePostCommand _model = new() { IsCommentsEnabled = true };
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _categories = new();
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _tags = new();
    private HashSet<int> _selectedTagIds = new();
    private bool _saving;
    private string? _error;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        _categories = await CategoriesLookup.Invoke(new GetCategoriesLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);
        _tags = await TagsLookup.Invoke(new GetTagsLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);
    }

    private void ToggleTag(int id, bool selected)
    {
        if (selected) _selectedTagIds.Add(id);
        else _selectedTagIds.Remove(id);
    }

    private async Task Submit()
    {
        _saving = true;
        _error = null;
        try
        {
            _model.TagIds = _selectedTagIds.ToList();
            var id = await CreateHandler.Invoke(_model, CancellationToken.None);
            Nav.NavigateTo($"/admin/posts/edit/{id}");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _saving = false;
        }
    }
}

            </button>
            <a href="/admin/posts" class="btn btn-light">انصراف</a>
        </div>
    </EditForm>

@code {
    [Parameter] public int Id { get; set; }

    private UpdatePostCommand? _model;
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _categories = new();
    private List<IdTitleDto<int>> _tags = new();
    private HashSet<int> _selectedTagIds = new();
    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _saving;
    private string? _error;
    private string? _success;
    private int _viewCount;
    private string _tinyUrl = "";

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        _loading = true;
        _error = null;
        _success = null;
        try
        {
            _categories = await CategoriesLookup.Invoke(new GetCategoriesLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);
            _tags = await TagsLookup.Invoke(new GetTagsLookupQuery(), CancellationToken.None);

            var post = await GetHandler.Invoke(new GetPostQuery { Id = Id }, CancellationToken.None);
            _model = new UpdatePostCommand
            {
                Id = post.Id,
                Title = post.Title,
                Slug = post.Slug,
                Summary = post.Summary,
                Content = post.Content,
                CoverImageUrl = post.CoverImageUrl,
                CategoryId = post.CategoryId,
                IsPublished = post.IsPublished,
                IsCommentsEnabled = post.IsCommentsEnabled,
                IsInEnglish = post.IsInEnglish,
                MetaTitle = post.MetaTitle,
                MetaDescription = post.MetaDescription,
                OgImageUrl = post.OgImageUrl,
                TagIds = post.TagIds
            };
            _selectedTagIds = post.TagIds.ToHashSet();
            _viewCount = post.ViewCount;
            _tinyUrl = post.TinyUrl;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _model = null;
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }

    private void ToggleTag(int id, bool selected)
    {
        if (selected) _selectedTagIds.Add(id);
        else _selectedTagIds.Remove(id);
    }

    private async Task Submit()
    {
        if (_model is null) return;
        _saving = true;
        _error = null;
        _success = null;
        try
        {
            _model.TagIds = _selectedTagIds.ToList();
            await UpdateHandler.Invoke(_model, CancellationToken.None);
            _success = "تغییرات ذخیره شد.";
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _error = ex.Message;
        }
        finally
        {
            _saving = false;
        }
    }
}

@using AppServices.Admin.Posts.Delete

@using PersonalBlog.Utilities.Dtos
@using PersonalBlog.Domain.Entities.Posts.Dtos
@inject IQueryHandler<GetPostListAsGridQuery, GridDataSourceResult<PostGridDto>> GridHandler
@inject ICommandHandler<DeletePostCommand, int> DeleteHandler
@inject NavigationManager Nav

<PageTitle>مطالب | ادمین</PageTitle>

<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4 flex-wrap gap-2">
    <h1 class="h3 mb-0">مدیریت مطالب</h1>
    <a href="/admin/posts/create" class="btn btn-success">+ مطلب جدید</a>
</div>

@if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_message))
{
    <div class="alert @(_isError ? "alert-danger" : "alert-success") alert-dismissible fade show">
        @_message
        <button type="button" class="btn-close" @onclick="() => _message = null"></button>
    </div>
}

@if (_loading)
{
    <div class="text-center py-5 text-muted">در حال بارگذاری...</div>
}
else
{
    <div class="card shadow-sm">
        <div class="card-body p-0">
            <div class="table-responsive">
                <table class="table table-hover table-striped mb-0 align-middle">
                    <thead class="table-light">
                        <tr>
                            <th style="width:60px">#</th>
                            <th>عنوان</th>
                            <th>دسته‌بندی</th>
                            <th>وضعیت</th>
                            <th>بازدید</th>
                            <th>تاریخ</th>
                            <th style="width:160px"></th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        @if (_items.Count == 0)
                        {
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="7" class="text-center text-muted py-4">مطلبی ثبت نشده است.</td>
                            </tr>
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            @foreach (var item in _items)
                            {
                                <tr>
                                    <td>@item.Id</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <div class="fw-semibold">@item.Title</div>
                                        <small class="text-muted">@item.Slug</small>
                                    </td>
                                    <td>@item.CategoryTitle</td>
                                    <td>
                                        @if (item.IsPublished)
                                        {
                                            <span class="badge bg-success">منتشر شده</span>
                                        }
                                        else
                                        {
                                            <span class="badge bg-secondary">پیش‌نویس</span>
                                        }
                                        @if (item.IsInEnglish)
                                        {
                                            <span class="badge bg-info text-dark">EN</span>
                                        }
                                    </td>
                                    <td>@item.ViewCount</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <small>@item.CreatedAtPersian</small>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="text-nowrap">
                                        <a class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary" href="/admin/posts/edit/@item.Id">ویرایش</a>
                                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-danger" @onclick="() => ConfirmDelete(item)" disabled="@_deleting">حذف</button>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            }
                        }
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
        @if (_totalPages > 1)
        {
            <div class="card-footer d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                <small class="text-muted">جمع: @_totals مورد</small>
                <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">
                    <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary" disabled="@(_page <= 1)" @onclick="() => LoadPage(_page - 1)">قبلی</button>
                    <span class="btn btn-outline-secondary disabled">@_page / @_totalPages</span>
                    <button class="btn btn-outline-secondary" disabled="@(_page >= _totalPages)" @onclick="() => LoadPage(_page + 1)">بعدی</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        }
    </div>
}

@code {
    private List<PostGridDto> _items = new();
    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _deleting;
    private int _page = 1;
    private int _pageSize = 15;
    private int _totalPages;
    private int _totals;
    private string? _message;
    private bool _isError;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync() => await LoadPage(1);

    private async Task LoadPage(int page)
    {
        _loading = true;
        _page = page;
        try
        {
            var result = await GridHandler.Invoke(new GetPostListAsGridQuery
            {
                Page = _page,
                PageSize = _pageSize
            }, CancellationToken.None);

            _items = result.Data?.ToList() ?? new();
            _totalPages = result.TotalPages;
            _totals = result.Totals;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _message = ex.Message;
            _isError = true;
            _items = new();
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task ConfirmDelete(PostGridDto item)
    {
        if (!await Task.FromResult(true)) return; // UI confirm simplified
        _deleting = true;
        try
        {
            await DeleteHandler.Invoke(new DeletePostCommand { Id = item.Id }, CancellationToken.None);
            _message = $"مطلب «{item.Title}» حذف شد.";
            _isError = false;
            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _message = ex.Message;
            _isError = true;
        }
        finally
        {
            _deleting = false;
        }
    }
}


=== FILE: src/Web/Components/Admin/Pages/Tags.razor ===
@page "/admin/tags"
@layout Web.Components.Admin.Layout.AdminLayout
@rendermode InteractiveServer

@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Create
@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Update
@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Delete
@using AppServices.Admin.Tags.Grid
@using AppServices.Base
@using PersonalBlog.Domain.Entities.Tags.Dtos
@using PersonalBlog.Utilities.Dtos

@inject IQueryHandler<GetTagListAsGridQuery, GridDataSourceResult<TagGridDto>> GridHandler
@inject ICommandHandler<CreateTagCommand, int> CreateHandler
@inject ICommandHandler<UpdateTagCommand, int> UpdateHandler
@inject ICommandHandler<DeleteTagCommand, int> DeleteHandler

<PageTitle>برچسب‌ها | ادمین</PageTitle>

@code {

    private List<TagGridDto> _items = new();

    private bool _loading = true;
    private bool _processing;

    private bool _showForm;
    private bool _editing;

    private string? _message;
    private bool _isError;

    private int _page = 1;
    private int _pageSize = 20;

    private int _totalItems;
    private int _totalPages;

    private CreateTagCommand _createModel = new();

    private UpdateTagCommand _updateModel = new();

    private object _form => _editing
        ? _updateModel
        : _createModel;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        await LoadPage(1);
    }

    private async Task LoadPage(int page)
    {
        _loading = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            GridDataSourceResult<TagGridDto> result = await GridHandler.Invoke(
                new GetTagListAsGridQuery
                {
                    Page = page,
                    PageSize = _pageSize
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            _items = result.Data?.ToList() ?? new();

            _page = page;

            _totalItems = result.Totals;

            _totalPages = (int)Math.Ceiling(
                (double)_totalItems / _pageSize);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _items = new();
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }

    private void ShowCreate()
    {
        _editing = false;

        _createModel = new CreateTagCommand();

        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }

    private void ShowEdit(TagGridDto item)
    {
        _editing = true;

        _updateModel = new UpdateTagCommand
        {
            Id = item.Id,
            Title = item.Title
        };

        _message = null;

        _showForm = true;
    }

    private void CloseForm()
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _showForm = false;
    }

    private async Task Save()
    {
        _processing = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            if (_editing)
            {
                await UpdateHandler.Invoke(
                    _updateModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);
            }
            else
            {
                await CreateHandler.Invoke(
                    _createModel,
                    CancellationToken.None);
            }

            _showForm = false;

            ShowSuccess(
                _editing
                    ? "برچسب با موفقیت ویرایش شد."
                    : "برچسب با موفقیت ایجاد شد.");

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }

    private async Task Delete(TagGridDto item)
    {
        if (_processing)
            return;

        _processing = true;
        _message = null;

        try
        {
            await DeleteHandler.Invoke(
                new DeleteTagCommand
                {
                    Id = item.Id
                },
                CancellationToken.None);

            ShowSuccess("برچسب با موفقیت حذف شد.");

            if (_items.Count == 1 && _page > 1)
                _page--;

            await LoadPage(_page);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowError(ex.Message);
        }
        finally
        {
            _processing = false;
        }
    }

    private void ShowSuccess(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = false;
    }

    private void ShowError(string message)
    {
        _message = message;
        _isError = true;
    }
}

.components-reconnect-first-attempt-visible,

=== FILE: src/Web/Components/Layout/ReconnectModal.razor.js ===
// Set up event handlers
const reconnectModal = document.getElementById("components-reconnect-modal");
reconnectModal.addEventListener("components-reconnect-state-changed", handleReconnectStateChanged);

const retryButton = document.getElementById("components-reconnect-button");
retryButton.addEventListener("click", retry);

const resumeButton = document.getElementById("components-resume-button");
resumeButton.addEventListener("click", resume);

function handleReconnectStateChanged(event) {
    if (event.detail.state === "show") {
        reconnectModal.showModal();
    } else if (event.detail.state === "hide") {
        reconnectModal.close();
    } else if (event.detail.state === "failed") {
        document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", retryWhenDocumentBecomesVisible);
    } else if (event.detail.state === "rejected") {
        location.reload();
    }
}

async function retry() {
    document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", retryWhenDocumentBecomesVisible);

    try {
        // Reconnect will asynchronously return:
        // - true to mean success
        // - false to mean we reached the server, but it rejected the connection (e.g., unknown circuit ID)
        // - exception to mean we didn't reach the server (this can be sync or async)
        const successful = await Blazor.reconnect();
        if (!successful) {
            // We have been able to reach the server, but the circuit is no longer available.
            // We'll reload the page so the user can continue using the app as quickly as possible.
            const resumeSuccessful = await Blazor.resumeCircuit();
            if (!resumeSuccessful) {
                location.reload();
            } else {
                reconnectModal.close();
            }
        }
    } catch (err) {
        // We got an exception, server is currently unavailable
        document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", retryWhenDocumentBecomesVisible);
    }
}

async function resume() {
    try {
        const successful = await Blazor.resumeCircuit();
        if (!successful) {
            location.reload();
        }
    } catch {
        reconnectModal.classList.replace("components-reconnect-paused", "components-reconnect-resume-failed");
    }
}

async function retryWhenDocumentBecomesVisible() {
    if (document.visibilityState === "visible") {
        await retry();
    }
}

<h3>Development Mode</h3>
<p>
    Swapping to <strong>Development</strong> environment will display more detailed information about the error that occurred.
</p>
<p>
    <strong>The Development environment shouldn't be enabled for deployed applications.</strong>
    It can result in displaying sensitive information from exceptions to end users.
    For local debugging, enable the <strong>Development</strong> environment by setting the <strong>ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT</strong> environment variable to <strong>Development</strong>
    and restarting the app.
</p>

=== FILE: src/Web/Components/Pages/Weather.razor ===
@page "/weather"
@attribute [StreamRendering]

<PageTitle>Weather</PageTitle>

<h1>Weather</h1>

<p>This component demonstrates showing data.</p>

@if (forecasts == null)
{
    <p><em>Loading...</em></p>
}
else
{
    <table class="table">
        <thead>
            <tr>
                <th>Date</th>
                <th aria-label="Temperature in Celsius">Temp. (C)</th>
                <th aria-label="Temperature in Fahrenheit">Temp. (F)</th>
                <th>Summary</th>
            </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
            @foreach (var forecast in forecasts)
            {
                <tr>
                    <td>@forecast.Date.ToShortDateString()</td>
                    <td>@forecast.TemperatureC</td>
                    <td>@forecast.TemperatureF</td>
                    <td>@forecast.Summary</td>
                </tr>
            }
        </tbody>
    </table>
}

@code {
    private WeatherForecast[]? forecasts;

    protected override async Task OnInitializedAsync()
    {
        // Simulate asynchronous loading to demonstrate streaming rendering
        await Task.Delay(500);

        var startDate = DateOnly.FromDateTime(DateTime.Now);
        var summaries = new[] { "Freezing", "Bracing", "Chilly", "Cool", "Mild", "Warm", "Balmy", "Hot", "Sweltering", "Scorching" };
        forecasts = Enumerable.Range(1, 5).Select(index => new WeatherForecast
        {
            Date = startDate.AddDays(index),
            TemperatureC = Random.Shared.Next(-20, 55),
            Summary = summaries[Random.Shared.Next(summaries.Length)]
        }).ToArray();
    }

    private class WeatherForecast
    {
        public DateOnly Date { get; set; }
        public int TemperatureC { get; set; }
        public string? Summary { get; set; }
        public int TemperatureF => 32 + (int)(TemperatureC / 0.5556);
    }
}

@inherits LayoutComponentBase
@inject IJSRuntime JS

<div class="site-wrapper @(_isDark ? "dark" : "")" data-theme="@(_isDark ? "dark" : "light")">
    <header class="sticky top-0 z-50 bg-white/80 dark:bg-gray-800/80 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700">
        <div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-3 flex items-center justify-between">
            <a href="/" class="text-xl font-bold text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400">وبلاگ شخصی</a>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                <button type="button" @onclick="ToggleTheme" class="p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 transition" aria-label="تغییر قالب">
                    @if (_isDark)
                    {
                        <span>☀️</span>
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        <span>🌙</span>
                    }
                </button>
                <button type="button" @onclick="ToggleMenu" class="p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700 md:hidden" aria-label="منو">
                    ☰
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
        @if (_menuOpen)
        {
            <nav class="md:hidden border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 px-4 py-3 space-y-2">
                <a href="/" class="block py-2 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200" @onclick="CloseMenu">خانه</a>
                <a href="/posts" class="block py-2 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200" @onclick="CloseMenu">مطالب</a>
                <a href="/about" class="block py-2 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-200" @onclick="CloseMenu">درباره من</a>
            </nav>
        }
    </header>

    <main class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 py-6 min-h-[70vh]">
        @Body
    </main>

    <footer class="border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 mt-12 py-8 text-center text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
        <div class="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4">
            <p>© @DateTime.Now.Year وبلاگ شخصی — همه حقوق محفوظ است.</p>
        </div>
    </footer>
</div>

@code {
    private bool _isDark;
    private bool _menuOpen;

    protected override async Task OnAfterRenderAsync(bool firstRender)
    {
        if (firstRender)
        {
            try
            {
                var theme = await JS.InvokeAsync<string>("eval", "localStorage.getItem('theme') || 'light'");
                _isDark = theme == "dark";
                await ApplyThemeClass();
                StateHasChanged();
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }

    private async Task ToggleTheme()
    {
        _isDark = !_isDark;
        await JS.InvokeVoidAsync("eval", $"localStorage.setItem('theme', '{(_isDark ? "dark" : "light")}')");
        await ApplyThemeClass();
    }

    private async Task ApplyThemeClass()
    {
        await JS.InvokeVoidAsync("eval", _isDark
            ? "document.documentElement.classList.add('dark')"
            : "document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark')");
    }

    private void ToggleMenu() => _menuOpen = !_menuOpen;
    private void CloseMenu() => _menuOpen = false;
}

        finally

@rendermode InteractiveServer

@inject IQueryHandler<GetPostBySlugQuery, GetPostBySlugResult> PostHandler

<PageTitle>@(_post?.Title ?? "مطالب") | وبلاگ شخصی</PageTitle>

@if (_loading)
{
    <div class="animate-pulse space-y-4">
        <div class="h-8 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded w-3/4"></div>
        <div class="h-4 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded w-1/2"></div>
        <div class="h-40 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded"></div>
    </div>
}
else if (_post is null)
{
    <div class="text-center py-16">
        <p class="text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">مطلب یافت نشد.</p>
        <a href="/" class="text-sky-600 mt-4 inline-block">بازگشت به خانه</a>
    </div>
}
else
{
    <article>
        <div class="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400">
            <a href="/" class="hover:text-sky-600">خانه</a>
            <span>/</span>
            <span>@_post.CategoryTitle</span>
        </div>

        <h1 class="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold mb-3 leading-tight">@_post.Title</h1>

        <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-6">
            @if (_post.PublishedAt.HasValue)
            {
                <span>@_post.PublishedAt.Value.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd")</span>
            }
            <span>•</span>
            <span>@_post.ViewCount بازدید</span>
        </div>

        @if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_post.CoverImageUrl))
        {
            <img src="@_post.CoverImageUrl" alt="@_post.Title" class="w-full rounded-2xl mb-6 object-cover max-h-80" />
        }

        <div class="prose prose-sm dark:prose-invert max-w-none mb-8 leading-relaxed">
            @((MarkupString)_post.Content)
        </div>

        @if (_post.Tags.Any())
        {
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-8">
                @foreach (var tag in _post.Tags)
                {
                    <span class="px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-full bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300">#@tag</span>
                }
            </div>
        }
    </article>
}

@code {
    [Parameter] public string Slug { get; set; } = default!;

    private GetPostBySlugResult? _post;
    private bool _loading = true;

    protected override async Task OnParametersSetAsync()
    {
        _loading = true;
        try
        {
            _post = await PostHandler.Invoke(new GetPostBySlugQuery { Slug = Slug }, CancellationToken.None);
        }
        catch
        {
            _post = null;
        }
        finally
        {
            _loading = false;
        }
    }
}
